New bounded #13 library provides owner-derived save/load under an exact captured
ready session/runtime fence. It validates returned owner/full payload, coalesces
identical/reentrant saves, rejects conflicting replay/mixed-owner/oversized
history, returns copied immutable ordered records and redacts unknown errors.

but never publish to a new session. Revoke/drain waits for accepted operations.

## Retained original command — core-20261026-retained-command

CapturedConversationSession now retains one private retry closure capturing the
original immutable typed request, plus an exact operation/JSON fingerprint and
safe retry eligibility. No tokens, caller-supplied replacement payload, new event,
reset/new-round operation, disk storage, automatic dispatch or transcript writer.
This is a same-process/same-ready-generation prerequisite, NOT durable recovery
across refresh/logout/process restart. Runtime must keep/revoke its existing flow.

canRetryRetained is an explicit trusted host capability only when captured identity/
readiness remains current, one unresolved original command exists, the latest exact
original attempt failed retryably, and actual wire/caller work has drained. It
fails closed during readiness reentry or another submission. retryRetained takes
no payload/event: explicitly delegates to the SAME start/turn/finish method with
the original DTO; existing phase/session/receipt fingerprint/capacity guards remain

# Metadata-only conversation receipt reference — Issue #17

`ConversationReceiptReference` is an additive pure Dart local format, version1.
It identifies an EXISTING receipt by trusted project origin, owner UUID, one of
start_conversation/conversation_turn/finish_conversation, and client event UUID.
Named constructors take the existing immutable typed requests; only the event
identity is retained. The three operation scopes remain distinct even with the
same event UUID. UUID spelling, including schema-permitted nil/case, is preserved.

There is no original request, Japanese text, scenario/session ID, transcript,
response, token, dispatch claim, quota or permission in the reference. No command
can be reconstructed or replayed from it. It supplies no authentication, receipt
existence, completion, ownership, cancellation or safe-new-event authority.
`matchesScope` compares only local project/account partition metadata, folding
logical owner UUID case and canonicalizing trusted HTTPS origin; it returns false
for malformed scope input. An attacker can construct matching metadata: a fresh
verified server identity/own-profile barrier and authenticated authorized lookup
are still required. No new endpoint or lookup transport is implemented.

The constructor accepts only a bounded HTTPS origin/root (no userinfo, path,
query, fragment, whitespace or non-ASCII configuration). Case/default443/root
aliases canonicalize; another host or nondefault port remains another partition.
IPv6 follows the existing vault origin convention. This checks shape, not DNS,
certificate trust or configured-project authority. Caller supplies trusted app
configuration and verified identity; never expose URL/key/owner input to learners.

`toJson` returns five fresh scalar fields. `fromJson` requires exactly those
keys, integer version1, a canonical origin, known operation and valid UUIDs;
unknown/nested/credential/payload fields are rejected. `encode`/`decode` define
ONE canonical local JSON encoding (including key order), capped at1024 UTF-8
bytes with a preliminary UTF-16 bound. Decoder parses once then requires exact
re-encoding: duplicate members, alternate escapes/order/whitespace and trailing
data fail. This rule applies only to this new local format, not frozen wire JSON.
Invalid parsing exposes one fixed FormatException with no source/offset/input.
Default toString omits every value; IDs/project metadata must not be logged.

## Scope and next integration

This is a testable identification primitive for future restart reconciliation.
It performs no I/O, secure storage, encryption, HTTP, UI, new UUID generation,
retry, dispatch, transcript collection, logout/expiry policy or automatic action.
Current retained-original route recovery remains memory-only/same generation.
No reference is captured/saved/loaded by the app yet, and no restart is restored.

Next integration needs a separately reviewed secure metadata store and lifecycle:
exact project+owner partitions, caller ownership, bounded pending capacity without
eviction/expiry that abandons uncertainty, write-before-first-dispatch, generation
guards, late-write/clear ordering and fixed storage errors. Credential storage is
not permission to store learner transcripts or plaintext fallback. Corrupt,
missing or wrong-scope metadata must never authorize replacement event dispatch.
Fresh verified account/profile, actual old-I/O drain and trusted server receipt
lookup must precede any restored state; metadata alone cannot restore readiness.
Lookup must not renew claims or call the AI provider again. Authenticated lookup,
secure OS bridge/restart, logout/refresh/account-switch failures and actual learner
proof remain outstanding. No public API/schema/migration/RLS/default/provider or
Auth strategy change is made; all14 hosted release groups remain pending.

## Verification

project/account partition and aliases/ports/IPv6, UUID spelling/nil, exact keys/
version/types, payload/token rejection, canonical/duplicate parsing, byte limits,
fixed errors and immutable/fresh exports/redacted toString. Existing conversation
request tests and fixture bytes remain unchanged. Execution evidence is recorded
in PROJECT_STATUS/PR41; pure tests are not SDK/storage/hosted learner acceptance.

## Caller-owned pending receipt store component — Issue #17

# Caller-owned pending receipt store component — Issue #17

`ConversationReceiptStore` manages ONE pending metadata reference per canonical
project/account partition. Caller must supply `SecureConversationReceiptBackend`;
there is no production backend, default, file/memory fallback, native channel or
application wiring in this increment. The port contract requires bounded secure
I/O, confirmed durable writes/clears and no sensitive logs. A type/interface does
not prove encryption, OS security or persistence. TEST ONLY memory ports exercise
the state machine; they must never become a learner storage backend.

`ConversationReceiptScope` reuses the reference codec's HTTPS/UUID validation,
canonicalizes origin and logical owner case, and supplies no authentication.
`reference.scope` is additive; original v1 JSON/codec/factories stay unchanged.
The separate target namespace is
`NihongoDaily.ConversationReceipt.v1|<origin>|<lowercase owner UUID>`.
It never shares the refresh-secret target. Existing Windows refresh-vault native
validation remains unchanged and does NOT accept this new namespace. A reviewed
OS implementation/probe is still needed; do not bypass it or use plaintext.

## Pending state and lifecycle

`read` returns a validated immutable reference/null from the exact partition.
`reserve` first reads existing storage. It writes only when empty, treats an
identical canonical reference as inert and rejects every different pending
reference with conflict. No replacement, capacity eviction, expiry, automatic
retry, completion inference or deletion of malformed/foreign data occurs. Exact
wire spelling remains conservative even within one logical scope. The future
host must confirm reservation BEFORE dispatch AND freshly check captured Auth/
generation; storage success/null/conflict is never mutation/retry authority.

Backend read/write failures permanently fence normal access with fixed errors;
corrupt, noncanonical, wrong-scope or unexpected typed values also fail closed.
No input/backend details escape errors or default toString. Wrong-scope reserve
input is rejected before I/O. Metadata still contains account/project/event IDs
and must not be logged. No token/transcript/original command is retained here.

All operations serialize before external backend reentry. `clearForSignOut`
permanently revokes immediately, then clears ONLY its exact partition after all
actual earlier I/O. Queued old operations do not reach the backend; late reads
cannot publish and late successful writes cannot reopen the store. Clear still
runs after an earlier write failure with uncertain persistence. Concurrent clear
calls share one operation; explicit retry after a failed clear is permitted but
never reopens access. This local clear is NOT server cancellation/receipt deletion
or permission to send a replacement event. No general pending-discard API exists.

`close` revokes immediately, preserves pending metadata, drains actual operations
and releases exclusive ownership afterward. It does not close the borrowed backend
or erase confirmed saved metadata. One isolate-local owner per canonical target
prevents two facade writers, including configuration/case aliases. This is NOT
cross-process CAS/locking or multi-device synchronization: caller must prevent
competing instances; a future secure OS adapter needs its own reviewed ownership
contract. Failed sign-out clear remains an explicit caller-observed error; close
does not turn it into successful logout or certify remote revocation.

## Verification and remaining work

Eleven new pure TEST ONLY backend cases cover immutable/no-overwrite reservations,
partition/namespace/owner exclusivity, corrupt/foreign data, read/write failures,
pending write then sign-out/queued work, close/preserve/actual drain, late read,
failed clear/explicit retry, serialized conflicting reservations and ambiguous
failed write then clear. Existing ten reference/seven request tests remain intact.
Execution evidence is recorded in PROJECT_STATUS/PR41.

# Native Windows receipt credential primitive — Issue #17

Independent receipt_credential_vault.cpp/.h uses Windows Credential Manager generic
credentials for bounded metadata, under NihongoDaily.ConversationReceipt.v1 only.
Auth refresh vault source/allowlist/namespace remains untouched. Targets bind one
HTTPS-origin shape (ASCII lowercase host authority chars, <=480) and lowercase
owner UUID, total<=600. Native checks shape, not DNS/verified Auth/canonical URI
semantics; trusted Dart scope normalization remains required. No learner URL input.

Reference validation accepts ONLY exact local v1 five-field JSON order, origin
matching target, logical owner UUID matching target, three operation names and an
exact UUID event. Owner/event spelling may retain uppercase; no arbitrary fields,
text/token/response/claim/escaped values/whitespace/trailing bytes. Max1024bytes.
This native grammar matches canonical Dart-produced scalar metadata; it is not a
general JSON parser, authentication, receipt lookup proof or dispatch authority.

Read validates credential type/blob bounds and full scope-bound payload. Missing
is distinct from corrupt/unavailable; unknown OS errors are fixed statuses. Invalid
write/target never invokes OS mutation. Temporary write/corrupt-read/raw OS buffers
are wiped before release; returned std::string/Dart values are not zeroization
proof. Clear is exact-target/idempotent. No enumeration, bulk deletion, logging,
plaintext fallback, expiry, pending eviction or cross-process CAS is installed.
The higher Dart store still owns single-writer/no-overwrite/lifecycle rules: the
raw OS write primitive itself replaces one slot and is not a reservation API.
Credential Manager is OS-user scoped, not protection from same-user processes.

New TEST ONLY receipt_vault_probe.cpp is compiled and called inside the existing
mandatory Windows credential probe. All original Auth assertions/build flags are
preserved; failure of receipt checks fails that step. PID-qualified .invalid test
slots only, bounded payloads, independent owner/project entries, invalid writes
preserve prior metadata, replace/read, raw corruption detection, idempotent clear,
Auth allowlist separation and final cleanup of all three slots. OS checks are
AUTHORED locally UNRUN; Windows is unavailable here. No new cloud/live credentials.

SupabaseAiTextTransport.lookupStartReceipt/lookupTurnReceipt/lookupFinishReceipt
now explicitly bind a supplied FULL original typed DTO to an own-account receipt
read. Trusted host must retain that DTO; five-scalar vault metadata cannot restore
it. The existing five-field lookupReceipt stays unchanged. No runtime/default/UI
reader is enabled by this increment; same-owned reader forwarding/capture remains
next. No commands or text are added to disk/logs or learner configuration.

Before HTTP: current captured SDK Session/token/profile generation and trusted
project/owner match, then operation/event association via strict matcher preflight.
Foreign scope maps UNAUTHORIZED; inconsistent original reference maps fixed
VALIDATION_FAILURE without HTTP. Fixed GET /rest/v1/operation_receipts with ONLY
user_id,operation,client_event_id,request_json,status,response_json, captured owner/
operation/event filters and limit2. Original DTO/text is NEVER in URL/query/body;
only original association is checked locally against the response. No caller table,
column/endpoint override, provider call, mutation, token writer or storage owner.

Use the SAME private _send/captured bearer/key/shared busy lane/deadline/abort/
1MiB/stream limits/no redirects/no-store/actual close drain as retained reads and
text/mutations. A private boolean selects exactly one of TWO fixed projections;
all prior operations keep their original request/response behavior. Pre/post read
and post strict decode current checks suppress late results. Six-field original
mismatch/legacy five-field row/malformed projection maps fixed INTERNAL_ERROR.
Unknown remains ambiguous, and even exact stored association proves neither
upstream provenance nor server reservation release/current transcript applicability.
No clear/reset/retry/redispatch/new-event or recovered conversation authority.

captured GET/empty body/bearer/key/no-store/no redirects/typed result; foreign scope/
original op/event before HTTP; changed/missing/extra request fields, foreign rows,
